# Evidence records and captions: the wording pass

Records editor, 3 October 2026. One pass over the wording of all 1,044 evidence records and all 169 image
captions. This file says what was changed, what was decided, and what was left for the orchestrator. The
evidence files were written between 04:56 and 07:16; the caption files at 06:30, and one of them again at
07:16. Last saves: `hw.json` 06:19, `fnaf123.json` 06:45, `fnaf4-sl.json` 06:52, `scott.json` 07:08,
`ffps-ucn.json` and `sb-mimic.json` 07:16. Nothing was changed after 07:16.

## Summary

- **950 fields changed in 611 places**: 918 fields in 579 records, and 32 captions.
- By field: `title` 243, `claim` 209, `location` 73, `notes` 357 (8 of them new `notes` fields), `quote` 36
  (words added, none removed), `caption` 32.
- By rule (a field changed under two rules counts under each, so the sum is 960): **A** labels and names 129,
  **B** a title, claim or note that said more than the record 692, **C** "printed", dates and staging 54,
  **D** captions 32 (about a dozen of them are label or name changes that rule A also covers), **E** the
  fact-checkers' and writers' lists 53 (36 of them `quote` fields extended).
- By file: `evidence/fnaf123.json` 86 fields in 63 records; `fnaf4-sl.json` 126 in 86; `ffps-ucn.json` 116 in 82;
  `hw.json` 138 in 89; `sb-mimic.json` 337 in 177; `scott.json` 115 in 82. Captions: `credits/fnaf123.json` 4,
  `fnaf4-sl.json` 6, `ffps-ucn.json` 5, `hw.json` 4, `sb-mimic.json` 12, `scott.json` 1.
- No other field of any record was touched: `id`, `game`, `kind`, `certainty`, `when`, `people`, `places`, `tags`,
  `related`, `url`, `verifiedVia`, `image`, `file` and `sourcePage` are as they were. A script compared every
  record with the committed copy to confirm it. No record was added, removed or moved.
- The twelve files keep their formatting (`json.dumps(indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)`, no trailing newline).
- `npx tsx scripts/validate.ts` ends `OK`. Its one warning at the last run, "entities used on evidence without a
  page: withered-bonnie(2), withered-chica(1), phantom-balloon-boy(1)", comes from a change to `people.ts` and
  the records' `people` fields, which I may not edit (section 3 below). The known warning about
  `fnaf4-night8-no-story-text` was no longer printed.
- Until 07:21 `git diff --stat -- src/data/evidence src/data/credits` listed the twelve files and nothing else
  of mine (958 insertions, 950 deletions). The other modified files in the worktree (`people.ts`, `relations.ts`,
  `familyTrees.ts`, and the chapter files `apparatus.ts`, `makers.ts`, `underground.ts`) are other people's. At
  no point did one of my files change under me.
- I committed nothing. The orchestrator committed the twelve files at 07:21 as `37856e3`, exactly as this report
  describes them: the working copies and that commit are identical, and "before" in the tables below is the
  commit before it (`3e780e2`), copies of which are in `files/evw/base/`.

## How the pass was made

Every record was read, in thirteen slices, and every caption. Thirteen read-only reviewers read the same slices
against the rulings; their findings were weighed one by one and are not applied blindly. Edits were written to a
ledger and applied by a script from the copies committed before the pass, so the pass can be replayed. The script refuses to
reword a phrase inside quotation marks in a `title` or `claim`, refuses to drop a word from a `quote`, and holds
a `quote` to 45 words. The ledger (976 edits), the script and the reviewers' reports are in
`files/evw/` beside this folder (`ledger.txt`, `apply.py`, `review-*.md`).

Words were checked against what is on file before they were written: the cached wiki transcripts in
`/tmp/wiki-cache/` (Special Delivery's emails, Security Breach's messages and CDs, Secret of the Mimic's logs,
mailboxes, reels and radio tables, Into the Pit's calls and items, Scott's archived posts and the interview
transcripts) and the images in `public/img/`. Several dozen images were opened. One image was fetched from the
wiki to check a detail the rulings had just reported viewed (decision 16).

A quote-check of the chapters against the records (the fact-checkers' `quotecheck.mts`) gives the same result
before and after this pass: no quotation in a chapter lost its record. No record whose `quote` a page prints
whole (52 quote blocks in the chapters, 14 lines of the Scott & Faith word list) had its `quote` changed.

## What I decided

These are the choices the brief left open. Each is applied the same way throughout.

1. **Names a game prints or speaks are kept** (R26, tier 1): Golden Freddy, Phone Guy, Purple Guy (FNaF World,
   Ultimate Custom Night), Springtrap, Fredbear, Vanny, Ennard, HandUnit, M.X.E.S., Crying Child (FNaF World's
   own character), and Vanessa where the game names the guard (dialogue, messages, the CDs' "Hello Vanessa").
2. **Spring Bonnie.** FNaF4 prints the name ("Mine is Spring Bonnie") and FNaF3's tape speaks it, so FNaF4
   records and captions and the tape records keep it. FNaF3's minigame figures, Pizzeria Simulator's Fruity Maze
   figure and Into the Pit's rabbit are "the yellow rabbit" or "a yellow costumed figure": no screen there names
   them.
3. **"The Mimic"** stays in Secret of the Mimic's records and its two ending captions (the name is the game's
   title and R26 puts it in tier 1). In Security Breach, RUIN and Help Wanted 2 records it is "the copying
   endoskeleton", "the endoskeleton" where the scene has just shown it, or "the voice on the Roxy-Talky" and "the
   Gregory voice" for the voice. Two layer reports list `sotm-redeemed-ending-david.jpg` as a caption with a
   label; I took out "David's room" and kept "the Mimic".
4. **People a page or scene does not name.** The comic pages of the rooftop and Redemption endings and RUIN's
   Safe Spot picture print no name: "a blonde woman" (the notes say whose cap, uniform and hair they are). Logs
   and reels in which no one names the speaker: "a voice speaking as Fiona", "an endoskeleton"; the wiki's
   "Fiona/M1" is in the notes. Into the Pit's minigame figure: "a purple figure".
5. **Tier-3 labels in notes.** The brief lets a note name a label when the note is about the label. Thirteen
   notes do ("'Tape Girl' is a fan and wiki label"). Round 4b of R26, written while I worked, says a tier-3 label
   stands "nowhere else" than `people.ts`; I read that as a rule for the theory's text and left these notes, and
   list them at the end in case it is meant for records too.
6. **What rests on the wiki says so in the claim.** Where a record's content is the wiki's description, summary
   or table, and nobody here has seen or heard it, the title or the first words of the claim now say "By the
   wiki's ..." or "(reported)". The `certainty` field is not mine; every such record is listed below.
7. **Readings are in the notes and marked** ("Reading:", "the usual reading", "is a reading", "the wiki's
   identification"). Flat conclusions in notes were re-worded that way; universals became "this archive has
   found" or "in the material checked".
8. **"Printed"** is used only of words seen on a screen in play or in a capture of play. A text read from an
   image file of a document or a texture is "read from the document image on the wiki" or "a poster texture".
   Special Delivery's email dates are "delivered to players". No title, claim, location, note or caption gives
   a year after 1987 as the story's date; the `when` labels still do (section 2 of the last part).
9. **Cut and unused content** says what the item is and that it cannot be reached in play; other records no
   longer cite it for or against anything (R27).
10. **A phrase in quotation marks in a title or claim was never reworded**, as the brief requires. Where the
    sentence around it had to change, it was rebuilt around the phrase. Phrases that look wrong are listed at the
    end.
11. **Quotes** were lengthened only from words on file, never for a record that a page prints whole, and never
    past 45 words. Where two passes of an interview's machine transcript differ on the words, the quote was left
    alone (`scott-2018-interview-fnaf4-too-broad`).
12. **`location`** fields: a remark about the place was moved outside the quotation marks; "hidden Sister
    Location room" (the wiki's name for the room where the CDs play) became "the hidden room found with the Faz
    Camera" in 16 fields.
13. **FNaF4's restaurant** is "the restaurant", not "the diner", in FNaF4 fields: the game prints no name for it.
14. **Remarks addressed to "the lead"** stood in a few notes where readers could see them ("the lead should
    keep one"); they were removed, and what they asked for is under "Duplicates" and "Records in the wrong file"
    below.
15. **Ending names** ("The Alley", "Redemption", "Fake ending", "Real ending") stay as the records had them. I
    could not tell from what is on file which of them a game prints.
16. **The "PROTOTYPE" foot** (`ruin-prototype-freddy-headless`). Round 4b reports it viewed. I opened the wiki's
    capture (File:Prototype-Label.png) and it is plainly legible on the sole, so the record's title and claim are
    back to their committed wording and the note now says where it was viewed.
17. **Scott's character guide** for Ultimate Custom Night is in the cache
    (`/tmp/wiki-cache/ffps-ucn/steam/ucn-character-guide.txt`) and prints "RXQ aka Shadow Bonnie", "Withered
    Chica", "Withered Bonnie", "JJ" and "Phantom Mangle". Four notes said these were fan or wiki labels; they now
    say that the names are not shown in FNaF2 or FNaF3 and that the guide uses them later.

## What I changed

The tables are grouped by file. "Before" and "after" are the field as committed and as it stands now. Short
fields are given whole. For a long `claim`, `notes` or `quote` the row gives the changed words with a few words
on either side, and `[…]` marks where the rest of the field is left out; a field changed in two places has two
rows, numbered (1/2), (2/2). "(no such field)" means the record had no `notes` before.

'She' is not named in the audio either; balloons and ice cream are Circus Baby's elsewhere in the game (sl-girl-daddy-let-me-play, sl-baby-ice-cream-story). There are just certain design choices that were made for these robots that we don't fully understand. We were hoping that you could shed some light on those.' | B |

The usual reading is that this is the real Dispatch, and that some or all of the Dispatch voice Arnold heard that night was not Fazbear's; where the impersonation begins is not stated. Compare RUIN, where a second Gregory voice breaks in near the end and says 'I've been trying to reach you all night!' (ruin-real-gregory-not-at-pizzaplex). | B |

| `scottgames-site-fnaf4-html-83` | notes | The digits are not explained anywhere by Scott. The change is his: the same tag had carried an 8 and a 7 since April, and it was edited to an 8 and a 3 just before the game with the 'Fredbear & Friends 1983' television screen came out. It is the earliest sign that the game was meant to be read against 1983 as well as 1987. In 2017 he listed being 'hung up on 1983 vs 1987' among the misunderstandings. The tag name keeps its own 7 ('descr7iption'). | No explanation of the digits by Scott was found. The same tag had carried an 8 and a 7 since April, and it had an 8 and a 3 by 21 July, two days before the game with the 'Fredbear & Friends 1983' television screen came out. The usual reading is that the 8 and 3 point to 1983 as the 8 and 7 pointed to 1987; Scott has not said so. In 2017 he listed being 'hung up on 1983 vs 1987' among the misunderstandings. The tag name keeps its own 7 ('descr7iption'). | B |

## Not changed, for the orchestrator

Everything here needs a field I may not edit, a new record, a capture, or a check in the game. Within each
group the items that a ruling or a chapter leans on come first.

  `hw-credits-tape-girl-qa` (the credit says "QA"; that this is the tapes' speaker is an inference no note
  explains), `hw2-glam-freddy-fazbear-technician` (a question from a character who says he cannot see the
  player), `ffps-security-puppet-assignment` (only the fail message is quoted; the rest is "Per the wiki"),
  `ffps-credits-william-afton` (the credit line is stated; which character the part is rests on who voices
  it), `sd-cpu-whole-new-personality` (the workshop description is from the wiki's game page),
  `hw-tape-girl-calls-it-the-anomaly`, `into-the-pit-yellow-rabbit-naming`, `security-breach-vanny-lines` and
  `security-breach-burntrap-name-not-in-game` (each records an absence found by this archive's own search),
  `scott-2018-interview-fnaf3-teaser-read-as-golden-freddy` (no quote, and none of Scott's words for his
  agreement), `scott-2018-interview-sl-made-to-ground-story` (the second half rests on "Mm-hmm. Yeah, right.").
- `sl-technicians-hanged` (shown): what is shown is a hanged figure in each room; that they are the two
  technicians is a small reading stated flat in title and claim. No capture is on file to word it from.
- `sl-private-room-keypad-1983` (shown, a lead record of R3): the monitors' change was seen in captures; the
  digits rest on the wiki's text and the captures' titles, since the keypad has no display.
- `sl-breaker-map-obsv-identification` (shown): the third sentence is a deduction from where the markers sit;
  the record beside it uses `implied` for the same kind of match.
- `ruin-ending-standard` (shown): the wiki lists a second ending to the line, and which take the game uses was
  not checked.
- `fw-android-port-clock-ending` (stated): the notes say both that the screen prints "bite of 87" and that the
  text was "not checked on screen".

**1c. `implied` used for something else.** `ffps-hry223-label` and `ffps-midnight-motorist-later-that-night`
(each an unchecked wiki report of a name that may be a file name, with a `quote` field that presents it as words
from the game; R27 lists the first as unverified and data-only); `hw2-springtrap-cutout-pq3-scream` (a wiki
report "not checked by ear"); `ucn-fredbear-lines` (the grade marks wording that "may be misquoted", and the
claim still puts two fragments in quotation marks); `ucn-summoned-performers`, `secret-of-the-mimic-mail-party-canceled`,
`secret-of-the-mimic-mail-something-below` and `scott-2018-wanted-to-finish-story` (each claim is now only
what is said, word for word; the implied reading is in the notes); `scott-2020-charlie-game-and-book-counterparts`
(the title carries Scott's sentence, not the inference); `hw-jeremy-self-injury-implied` (the reading is the
whole claim, joined from three tapes that tape 1 says "may not be in order"); `fnaf4-flatline` (the grade was
for "the child dies", which is not in the claim; the tone itself is the wiki's report).

### 2. `when`: labels, precision and bases

These are the most visible faults left, because the interface draws `precision: "exact"` as "Printed" and sorts
by `when.year`.

- **Special Delivery, 28 records**: `when.year` 2019 or 2020 and labels such as "Special Delivery's present (c.
  2019-2020)" or "c. <date>, 2019 (day the email reached players ...)". R24: delivery days; no year after 1987
  is in the story. `sd-ffs-never-alone-ad`, `sd-tutorial-unit-eternal-package` and
  `sd-springtrap-did-not-save-the-others` do not even say that the game gives no year. `sd-mail-server-advisory`
  ("launch period, 2019") and `sd-mangle-original-restaurants` (label says "sent when the Mangle joined the
  service"; its basis says the wiki gives no date) disagree with their own bases.
- **Secret of the Mimic**: 29 records carry "Secret of the Mimic present night (work order dated 9/07/79)",
  and 33 of the game's records have precision `exact`. The work order was read from an image file and is not
  confirmed in play (R2, R24, R27; `secret-of-the-mimic-teaser-1979` says so).
  `secret-of-the-mimic-mail-foreclosure` is labelled "3 August 1979", one of the two readings its own notes
  give. The memo, chart and order-form records say "date typed on the memo", "years printed on the chart".
- **Into the Pit, 25 records**: `year: 1985` and "1985 (the past reached through the ball pit)", on one logo
  graphic whose screen was not confirmed (R23: the logo dates nothing).
- **Staged props**: `hw2-fall-fest-1970-poster` ("1970 (year printed on the poster)", exact, basis "it dates
  the fair it advertises") and `codb-fallfest-83-banner` ("the banner prints 83 ... this dates the fair
  depicted"). R27: a prop in a company product dates nothing and is never "printed".
- **Help Wanted 2, 48 records**: "During Help Wanted 2 (undated; after Security Breach's night and before
  RUIN)", basis "... Roxanne is shattered as she is after Security Breach, and the main ending cuts to the scene
  that opens RUIN". It uses the fan word "shattered" and gives "before RUIN" flat, though `hw2-faz-force-ending`
  was not watched.
- **Readings in labels**: `fnaf1-news-closing` ("Year of FNaF1": the clipping is undated; R12 has it at medium);
  "Past event depicted in a FNaF3 minigame (undated)" on the cake minigames and Happiest Day, and "Undated past
  event depicted in an FFPS minigame" on Fruity Maze and the three Security Puppet records (nothing in the
  records marks the scenes as past; R14); `fnaf3-tapes-freddy-fazbears-pizza` ("Springlock era (before the 1987
  location)", which its notes call a reading); `fnaf2-cutscenes-old-stage-through-eye-holes` ("The room is not
  the FNaF2 location", firmer than its notes); "After the Bite (FNaF4 Night 6 cutscene), c. 1983" on five
  records (close to the label R26 retires); "Custom Night cutscenes depict the aftermath of the Real Ending"
  (three records) and "Cutscene shows the burned remains of Fazbear's Fright behind the speaker" (six; the sign
  is partly legible, "FAZBE... / FRI..."); `scott-ucn-store-once-again-trapped` ("During UCN", for marketing
  copy); the four `scottgames-site-sl-news-*` records ("Before Sister Location", which the report does not say).
- **Small disagreements**: `fnaf4-mangle-pieces-in-house` is labelled "4 days until the party" and its location
  gives the 2-days minigame; `fnaf4-fredbear-and-friends-1983` is "1983 (exact)" where the seven other records
  of the same minigame are "c. 1983 (approx)"; `scottgames-site-2021-retirement-not-end` and
  `scottgames-site-2015-no-phone-numbers` say "posted" with a date their location gives as "captured";
  `scott-2017-retcon-seamless` has a step of reasoning in its basis.

### 4. Needs a capture, or a check in the game

From round4.md (sections 3 and 5.3), still open as far as the records go: the fifth body in Give Gifts, Give
Life; FNaF4's back-room figure and its chest mark; whether Burn It All Down shows the pizzeria collapse; the
scenes recorded from summaries (Princess Quest II and III, Help Wanted 2's main ending, the shadow in RUIN's
hideout, the wrecked Bonnie's room); the work order and credential form in play; RUIN's costumes beside Secret
of the Mimic's; the two yellow prototypes' look; the second voice under three rare UCN lines. **Done since**:
the "PROTOTYPE" foot (decision 16).

Found in this pass:

- `fnaf3-happiest-day`: the claim and the notes tell the scene in a different order (claim: the child already
  wears the golden mask and "a cake appears"; notes: the Puppet-masked child brings the cake and the mask goes
  on then). The still on file cannot settle it.
- `security-breach-ending-alley`: the image on file is the first page. The silhouette over Gregory is on a
  second page that is not on file; that part of the claim follows the wiki's caption.
- `hw-mobile-princess-quest-graveyard`: needs a capture from the Help Wanted mobile port.
- `sl-private-room-keypad-1983`: the digits, on a readable frame.
- Secret of the Mimic: where F10-N4's imitation of Dispatch begins. Three records disagreed and all now say it
  is not stated (`secret-of-the-mimic-dispatch-contractor-murray`, `-dispatch-endoskeleton-any-costume`,
  `-clock-out-real-dispatch`); R2 and R31 treat the opening call as the company's own. Also: who or what speaks
  "Your parents love you. Sleep tight." in the Story Time ending (the line is in the wiki's F10-N4 table; the
  claim gives it to "a Fiona recording"); the bodies in suits of `secret-of-the-mimic-previous-technicians`; what
  was compared for `secret-of-the-mimic-mimic-ruin-appearance-link`; the Moon.EXE doll text; the Fredbear's
  poster in play.
- Help Wanted 2: `hw2-roxy-repair-mask` and `hw2-glitchtrap-corrupts-backstage` call one level "Repair Roxy"
  and "Salon: Roxy Repair"; whether Princess Quest IV names "the sword of light" (`hw2-pq4-old-man`,
  `hw2-pq4-player-enters-game` and `hw2-pq4-alpha-take-it` describe the moment differently); whether RUIN's
  Helpi says "the entity" (no Helpi transcript is on file; `hw2-mimic-teaser-mxes-ending` now says only what is).
- Security Breach: what in the hidden room recalls Sister Location; whether the game prints the endings' names;
  the post-credits newspapers of the Getaway and rooftop endings.
- RUIN: `ruin-cassie-seeks-gregory` says "Gregory's best friend", which is the store synopsis; no line in the
  cached audio calls her that, and chapters use "his best friend".
- Curse of Dreadbear: `codb-purple-headlights` describes the headlights in the present tense, while
  `codb-dancing-glitchtrap` says (per the wiki) that the dancing figure replaced that easter egg.
- Into the Pit: the screen that carries the 1985 logo; the Scooper ending of RUIN.
- FNaF World: `fw-hard-ending-killing-the-storyteller` and `fw-normal-ending-still-in-control` call the last
  boss "Scott's avatar"; no record says what the game shows to make him so. `fw-android-port-clock-ending`
  mentions Scott's Game Jolt post of 17 January 2017 and cites no record.
- `ffps-fruity-maze-decay`: "yellow rabbit figures" stands for the old "Spring Bonnie figures" by the archive's
  vocabulary; no capture of the third play is on file to check their look.

### 5. Records the reports ask for, and what I found for them

- **Security Breach's cast credits**: the draft is in `$NOTES/new-record-sb-credits.json`; it is not in the
  evidence files (not mine to add).
- **Secret of the Mimic** (round4.md 3.1 to 3.4, 5.3.6): that the cabinet F10-N4 is the first machine (the
  nearest record is `secret-of-the-mimic-mail-received-5-min-ago`, unsigned); the Murrays' marriage; David's
  death; the two prototypes' look; what the Data Diver is. A mailbox message "Please Advise" has "The orders are
  drying up." and has no record; the note of `secret-of-the-mimic-mail-group-chat-turning-down-work` now quotes
  it against its own old conclusion.
- **A name with a quoted line** (round4.md 5.3.3). Two are now on record: "HandUnit" (the quote of
  `sl-handunit-intro-job-ad` now ends "you can call me HandUnit.") and "Arnold" with "dispatch" (the opening
  call, "Arnold. Hey buddy, this is dispatch with Fazbear Entertainment. How are you feeling?", is transcribed in
  the notes of `secret-of-the-mimic-dispatch-contractor-murray`; its quote is printed whole by `makers.ts` and
  could not be lengthened). For the others:
  - **"F10-N4"**: in everything cached, the name appears only in the wiki's prose ("dubbed F10-N4") and in sound
    file names ("Dispatch F10N4 005.ogg"). No spoken line or screen with the name is on file. If no screen
    prints it, it is a file label, like "Burntrap", and R26 has it in tier 1. 29 fields in 16 records use it.
  - **"Oswald"**: spoken in the game. The cached wiki page `Phone.wiki` has Mom: "Oswald? Is everything alright
    honey?" and Jeff: "No. Bye, Oswald." No record covers those calls, so it needs a new one.
  - "Glamrock Freddy", "Montgomery Gator", "Glamrock Chica": not in any cached message, CD or line; the quotes
    have "Freddy", "Monty", "Montgomery" and "Chica".
- **Scott's character guide** (round4.md 5.3.2): the text is in the cache (decision 17). It prints "RXQ aka
  Shadow Bonnie", so R26's tier 3 has one name that Scott used; also "Withered Chica", "Withered Bonnie", "JJ",
  "Phantom Mangle", "Phantom Freddy", "Phantom BB", "Dee Dee", "Nightmare Fredbear & Nightmare". Only the line
  for Afton has a record.
- **Scott's joke of 4 September 2017** (comment dmj4bft, the night before "The retcon issue"): "All planned. /
  All along. / From the beginning of time. Never had to retcon anything." It is in the archived comments and in
  the notes of `scott-2017-one-retcon`; the pages wanted to quote it and it has no record of its own.
- **That the Pizzaplex closes with Gregory inside** (revise-lead.md), and **that the building RUIN shows is the
  underground route's** (layerfix-time.md): no record, and I found nothing on file that could be one.
- **Whether Special Delivery's inbox showed a date beside each email** (round4.md 5.3.7): the records still say
  it was not verified.

- **Round 4b of R26 and the notes.** These notes name a tier-3 label in order to say whose name it is:
  `fnaf2-shadow-freddy`, `fnaf2-shadow-bonnie-office`, `fnaf3-eon-shadow-freddy-follow-me`,
  `fnaf3-shadow-bonnie-minigame`, `fnaf3-office-slumped-bear`, `hw-tape-01`, `hw-credits-tape-girl-qa`,
  `hw2-mimic-teaser-mxes-ending`, `security-breach-ending-burn-it-all-down`, `security-breach-tangle-composition`,
  `security-breach-shattered-glamrocks-underground`, `ruin-the-entity`, `ruin-something-tunneled`. If "nowhere
  else" covers record notes, these thirteen are the places. No tier-3 label is left in any title, claim,
  location or caption.
- **Names from the novels**: none is in a visible field except as Scott's or a product's own word, in quotation
  marks: "Cassidy" (`scott-2020-cassidy-kazoo-names-from-lore`, `scott-2020-cassidy-screenplay-lore-from-games`)
  and "Charlie" (`scott-2020-charlie-game-and-book-counterparts`). A surname from the novels stood in two notes
  as the wiki's naming (`into-the-pit-strange-photo`, `secret-of-the-mimic-log-build-to-spec`); both now say "a
  surname from the novels". A book fact stood in a claim: `into-the-pit-new-mill-fathers` said "in the present
  the mill has closed"; the game text on file says only that Dad lost his job. Removed there and from the title
  of `into-the-pit-mill-closure`.
- **`secret-of-the-mimic-log-death` does not name Fiona.** Edwin speaks to "you" and of "his mother"; the log
  is titled "Death". Round four cites this record for "Fiona, his son's dead mother", and the storyline's "Fiona
  dies" cites it alone. Title and claim now give the log's words, the quote has its opening sentences, and the
  note names the recordings that join her to it (`secret-of-the-mimic-log-installation-worries`,
  `secret-of-the-mimic-film-mommy-gone`). The join is a small one, and it is the model's.
- **"Fake ending" and "Real ending"** (Sister Location) stand as names in several titles, about ten locations and
  several `when` labels; no record shows the game or Scott using either. If they are ruled players' names, the
  titles should say so, as FNaF3's "good ending" titles now do.
- **`sl-custom-night-decay`**: the title still says the body "decays", as the orchestrator worded it; the claim
  records a change of colour and a stoop.
- **`ucn-death-coin-fredbear`, `ucn-fredbear-lines`**: no record says where the name "Fredbear" for UCN's secret
  figure comes from. Round four keeps the name.
- **`ffps-completion-not-called-by-who-you-assume`**: the claim says "tells Elizabeth"; the name is spoken
  earlier in the same speech, in another record's quote.
- **The wiki's term "memory doll(s)"** is used unquoted in five records; the game's word is "memory"
  (`hw2-memory-dolls` says so).
- **Chapters may quote "board member"** for Sister Location's first speaker (`family.ts`, `story.ts`,
  `machines.ts`); it is the wiki's label, and the audio gives him no role.

## Appendix: changed records that each chapter file cites

For whoever compares chapter sentences with the records. A record is listed if its `title`, `claim` or `quote`
changed (362 records) and the file cites it. Records whose `notes` or `location` alone changed are not
listed. Writer 1 re-read `story.ts` and `apparatus.ts` against the records as they stood at 06:08, and the lead
re-read the beats of `theory.ts`; the other chapter files were committed before the records changed. The pages
layer compared against the records as saved at 06:19, the people layer up to 07:08.
`$NOTES/tools/changed-records.py` prints the same lists with the old and new text, against any commit.
